    function loadState() {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
      if (saved) {
        try {
          appState = { ...appState, ...JSON.parse(saved) };
        } catch (e) {
          console.error('Erreur lecture cache local', e);
        }
      }
    }

    function saveState() {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(appState));
    }

    /* =====================================================================
       5. SÉLECTION D'ALIMENT & MOTEUR CULINAIRE
       ===================================================================== */
    let selectedFoodObj = EXTENDED_FOOD_DATABASE[0]; // Blanc de poulet par défaut

    // Fonctions Convertisseur Cru ⇄ Cuit
    function toggleCookedRawConverter() {
      const panel = document.getElementById('cookedRawConverterPanel');
      panel.classList.toggle('hidden');
    }

    function convertPatesCookedToRaw() {
      const val = Number(document.getElementById('foodGramsInput').value) || 250;
      const rawVal = Math.round(val / 2.5);
      document.getElementById('foodGramsInput').value = rawVal;
      toggleCookedRawConverter();
      updateFoodPreview();
    }

    function convertRizCookedToRaw() {
      const val = Number(document.getElementById('foodGramsInput').value) || 300;
      const rawVal = Math.round(val / 3.0);
      document.getElementById('foodGramsInput').value = rawVal;
      toggleCookedRawConverter();
      updateFoodPreview();
    }

    function convertMeatCookedToRaw() {
      const val = Number(document.getElementById('foodGramsInput').value) || 150;
      const rawVal = Math.round(val / 0.8);
      document.getElementById('foodGramsInput').value = rawVal;
      toggleCookedRawConverter();
      updateFoodPreview();
    }

    // Gestion Hydratation & Électrolytes
    function addWater(ml, type = 'eau') {
      appState.waterIntakeMl = (appState.waterIntakeMl || 0) + ml;
      if (type === 'electro') {
        // Boost électrolytes dans les marqueurs
        const potassiumLog = appState.medicalWatchlist.find(m => m.name.toLowerCase().includes('potassium'));
        if (potassiumLog) potassiumLog.current += 150;
      }
      saveState();
      renderWaterUI();
    }

    function resetWater() {
      appState.waterIntakeMl = 0;
      saveState();
      renderWaterUI();
    }

    function renderWaterUI() {
      const current = appState.waterIntakeMl || 0;
      const target = 3000;
      const pct = Math.min(100, Math.round((current / target) * 100));
      document.getElementById('waterTextTotal').textContent = `${current.toLocaleString()} ml`;
      document.getElementById('barWaterProgress').style.width = `${pct}%`;
      document.getElementById('navWaterVal').textContent = `${(current / 1000).toFixed(1)} / ${(target / 1000).toFixed(1)} L`;
    }

    // Gestion Jeûne Intermittent (16/8)
    function startFastingNow() {
      appState.fasting = {
        lastMealTimestamp: Date.now(),
        isFasting: true
      };
      saveState();
      updateFastingTimerUI();
    }

    function breakFastNow() {
      if (appState.fasting) {
        appState.fasting.isFasting = false;
      }
      saveState();
      updateFastingTimerUI();
    }

    function updateFastingTimerUI() {
      if (!appState.fasting || !appState.fasting.lastMealTimestamp) {
        document.getElementById('fastingHoursDisplay').textContent = '--';
        return;
      }

      const now = Date.now();
      const elapsedMs = Math.max(0, now - appState.fasting.lastMealTimestamp);
      const totalHours = Math.floor(elapsedMs / (3600 * 1000));
      const totalMins = Math.floor((elapsedMs % (3600 * 1000)) / (60 * 1000));

      document.getElementById('fastingHoursDisplay').textContent = `${totalHours}h ${totalMins}m`;

      const statusBadge = document.getElementById('fastingTimerStatus');
      if (totalHours >= 16) {
        statusBadge.textContent = 'Objectif 16h Atteint !';
        statusBadge.className = 'text-xs font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30';
      } else {
        statusBadge.textContent = appState.fasting.isFasting ? 'Jeûne en cours' : 'Fenêtre d\'alimentation';
        statusBadge.className = 'text-xs font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-purple-950 text-purple-300 border border-purple-500/30';
      }

      const d = new Date(appState.fasting.lastMealTimestamp);
      document.getElementById('fastingLastMealTime').textContent = `${d.getHours().toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${d.getMinutes().toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
    }

    // Modal Pesée & Mensurations
    function openWeightLogModal() {
      document.getElementById('inputNewWeight').value = appState.profile.weight || 78;
      document.getElementById('inputNewWaist').value = appState.waistCircumference || 81.5;
      document.getElementById('weightLogModal').classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function closeWeightLogModal() {
      document.getElementById('weightLogModal').classList.add('hidden');
    }

    function handleSaveWeightLog(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const newWeight = parseFloat(document.getElementById('inputNewWeight').value);
      const newWaist = parseFloat(document.getElementById('inputNewWaist').value);

      if (newWeight > 0) appState.profile.weight = newWeight;
      if (newWaist > 0) appState.waistCircumference = newWaist;

      saveState();
      closeWeightLogModal();
      renderAll();
    }

    // Modal Favoris
    function openFavoritesModal() {
      const list = document.getElementById('favoritesList');
      if (!appState.favoriteMeals || appState.favoriteMeals.length === 0) {
        list.innerHTML = `<p class="text-slate-500 italic py-4 text-center">Aucun repas favori enregistré.</p>`;
      } else {
        list.innerHTML = appState.favoriteMeals.map((fav, idx) => `
          <div class="p-2.5 bg-slate-900 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 space-y-1.5">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
              <strong class="text-slate-200 text-xs">${fav.name}</strong>
              <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <button onclick="loadFavoriteMeal(${idx})" class="px-2 py-0.5 bg-brand-500/20 text-brand-300 border border-brand-500/30 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold hover:bg-brand-500/30">
                  + Ajouter au journal
                </button>
                <button onclick="deleteFavoriteMeal(${idx})" class="text-slate-500 hover:text-red-400 text-xs p-1">
                  <i class="fa-solid fa-trash-can"></i>
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
            <p class="text-slate-400 text-[10px]">${fav.items.map(i => `${i.name} (${i.amount})`).join(', ')}</p>
          </div>
        `).join('');
      }
      document.getElementById('favoritesModal').classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function closeFavoritesModal() {
      document.getElementById('favoritesModal').classList.add('hidden');
    }

    function saveCurrentMealAsFavorite() {
      if (appState.dailyLogs.length === 0) {
        alert('Votre journal est vide aujourd\'hui ! Ajoutez d\'abord des aliments.');
        return;
      }
      const mealName = prompt('Nom de ce repas type / favori :', 'Mon Déjeuner Équilibré');
      if (!mealName) return;

      if (!appState.favoriteMeals) appState.favoriteMeals = [];
      appState.favoriteMeals.push({
        id: 'fav_' + Date.now(),
        name: mealName,
        items: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(appState.dailyLogs))
      });

      saveState();
      alert(`Repas "${mealName}" mémorisé dans vos favoris !`);
    }

    function loadFavoriteMeal(idx) {
      const fav = appState.favoriteMeals[idx];
      if (!fav) return;

      fav.items.forEach(item => {
        appState.dailyLogs.push({
          ...item,
          id: 'food_' + Date.now() + Math.random()
        });
      });

      saveState();
      closeFavoritesModal();
      renderAll();
    }

    function deleteFavoriteMeal(idx) {
      appState.favoriteMeals.splice(idx, 1);
      saveState();
      openFavoritesModal();
    }

    // Export / Import Backup JSON
    function exportDataJSON() {
      const dataStr = "data:text/json;charset=utf-8," + encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(appState, null, 2));
      const downloadAnchor = document.createElement('a');
      downloadAnchor.setAttribute("href", dataStr);
      downloadAnchor.setAttribute("download", `nutritracker_backup_${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.json`);
      document.body.appendChild(downloadAnchor);
      downloadAnchor.click();
      downloadAnchor.remove();
    }

    function triggerImportJSON() {
      document.getElementById('importFileInput').click();
    }

    function handleFileImport(event) {
      const file = event.target.files[0];
      if (!file) return;

      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = function(e) {
        try {
          const imported = JSON.parse(e.target.result);
          if (imported.profile) {
            appState = imported;
            saveState();
            renderAll();
            alert('Données restaurées avec succès !');
          } else {
            alert('Format de fichier de sauvegarde invalide.');
          }
        } catch (err) {
          alert('Erreur lors de la lecture du fichier JSON.');
        }
      };
      reader.readAsText(file);
    }

    /* =====================================================================
       MODULE IA : ANALYSE DE RECETTE COPÌÉE-COLLÉE
       ===================================================================== */
    let parsedAIRecipeIngredients = [];

    function insertExampleRecipe(type) {
      const area = document.getElementById('aiRecipeTextInput');
      if (type === 1) {
        area.value = "180g de filet de poulet cuit sauté\n120g de pâtes au blé dur crues\n80g de champignons de Paris\n2 gousses d'ail frais (10g)\n25g de parmesan AOP\n1 cuillère à soupe d'huile d'olive (15g)";
      } else if (type === 2) {
        area.value = "40g de flocons d'avoine complète\n30g d'Isolat de Whey Native Nutrimuscle\n1 banane fraîche (120g)\n200ml de boisson d'amande\n1 cuillère à soupe de graines de chia (15g)";
      }
    }

    async function analyzeRecipeWithAI() {
      const text = document.getElementById('aiRecipeTextInput').value.trim();
      if (!text) {
        alert('Veuillez d\'abord coller ou écrire une recette !');
        return;
      }

      const btn = document.getElementById('btnAnalyzeRecipe');
      const originalText = btn.innerHTML;
      btn.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Décomposition IA en cours...`;
      btn.disabled = true;

      // Parsing local intelligent immédiat avec notre base étendue d'aliments
      try {
        const lines = text.split('\n').filter(l => l.trim().length > 0);
        const parsed = [];
        const allDb = getAllFoods();

        lines.forEach(line => {
          const cleanLine = line.toLowerCase();
          
          // Détection grammage (ex: 200g, 500g, 150 ml, bocal de 500g, 2 gousses, etc.)
          let grams = 100;
          const gramMatch = cleanLine.match(/(\d+)\s*(g|gr|grammes|ml)/);
          const cuillereMatch = cleanLine.match(/(\d+)?\s*(c\.?\s*à\s*s\.?|cuill[eè]re)/);
          const gousseMatch = cleanLine.match(/(\d+)\s*(gousse)/);
          const bocalMatch = cleanLine.match(/(?:bocal|bo[iî]te|pot|conserve)\s*(?:de)?\s*(\d+)\s*(?:g|gr)?/);

          if (bocalMatch && bocalMatch[1]) {
            grams = parseInt(bocalMatch[1], 10);
          } else if (gramMatch) {
            grams = parseInt(gramMatch[1], 10);
          } else if (cuillereMatch) {
            grams = 15; // 1 c. à s. standard
          } else if (gousseMatch) {
            grams = parseInt(gousseMatch[1], 10) * 5; // ~5g par gousse
          }

          // Match le meilleur aliment de la base
          let matchedFood = null;
          let bestScore = 0;

          allDb.forEach(food => {
            const foodWords = food.name.toLowerCase().split(' ');
            let score = 0;
            foodWords.forEach(w => {
              if (w.length > 2 && cleanLine.includes(w)) score += 2;
            });
            if (cleanLine.includes(food.id)) score += 5;

            if (score > bestScore) {
              bestScore = score;
              matchedFood = food;
            }
          });

          // Si aucun aliment précis n'est matché, estimation générique saine
          if (!matchedFood || bestScore < 2) {
            matchedFood = {
              name: line.replace(/^\d+\s*(g|ml)?\s*(de)?\s*/i, '').trim(),
              per100g: { cals: 120, p: 5, c: 15, f: 3 },
              micros: {}
            };
          }

          const factor = grams / 100;
          parsed.push({
            name: matchedFood.name,
            originalLine: line,
            grams: grams,
            cals: Math.round(matchedFood.per100g.cals * factor),
            p: Number((matchedFood.per100g.p * factor).toFixed(1)),
            c: Number((matchedFood.per100g.c * factor).toFixed(1)),
            f: Number((matchedFood.per100g.f * factor).toFixed(1)),
            micros: matchedFood.micros || {}
          });
        });

        parsedAIRecipeIngredients = parsed;
        renderAIRecipeResults();

      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Erreur analyse recette', err);
        alert('Erreur lors du calcul de la recette.');
      } finally {
        btn.innerHTML = originalText;
        btn.disabled = false;
      }
    }

    function renderAIRecipeResults() {
      const container = document.getElementById('aiRecipeResultsList');
      const badge = document.getElementById('aiRecipeCountBadge');
      const card = document.getElementById('aiRecipeTotalCard');

      if (parsedAIRecipeIngredients.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = `<p class="text-slate-500 italic text-[11px] py-4 text-center">Aucun ingrédient détecté.</p>`;
        card.classList.add('hidden');
        badge.textContent = `0 ingrédient`;
        return;
      }

      badge.textContent = `${parsedAIRecipeIngredients.length} ingrédients extraits`;

      let totCals = 0, totP = 0, totC = 0, totF = 0;

      container.innerHTML = parsedAIRecipeIngredients.map(item => {
        totCals += item.cals;
        totP += item.p;
        totC += item.c;
        totF += item.f;

        return `
          <div class="p-2 bg-slate-800/80 rounded-lg border border-slate-700/60 flex items-center justify-between text-[11px]">
            <div>
              <span class="font-bold text-slate-200 block">${item.name}</span>
              <span class="text-[10px] text-slate-400">${item.grams}g • ${item.cals} kcal</span>
            </div>
            <div class="text-right text-[10px]">
              <span class="text-brand-400 font-semibold">P: ${item.p}g</span> | 
              <span class="text-accentCyan font-semibold">G: ${item.c}g</span> | 
              <span class="text-accentRose font-semibold">L: ${item.f}g</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }).join('');

      document.getElementById('aiTotalCals').textContent = totCals;
      document.getElementById('aiTotalProt').textContent = `${totP.toFixed(1)}g`;
      document.getElementById('aiTotalCarbs').textContent = `${totC.toFixed(1)}g`;
      document.getElementById('aiTotalFats').textContent = `${totF.toFixed(1)}g`;

      card.classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function addAIRecipeToJournal() {
      if (parsedAIRecipeIngredients.length === 0) return;

      parsedAIRecipeIngredients.forEach(item => {
        appState.dailyLogs.push({
          id: 'ai_food_' + Date.now() + Math.random(),
          name: item.name,
          meal: 'Plat Recette IA',
          amount: `${item.grams}g`,
          cookingNote: '🤖 Analyseur Recette',
          cals: item.cals,
          p: item.p,
          c: item.c,
          f: item.f,
          micros: item.micros
        });
      });

      // Mettre à jour l'heure de dernier repas pour le jeûne
      if (appState.fasting) {
        appState.fasting.lastMealTimestamp = Date.now();
      }

      saveState();
      renderAll();
      switchTab('dashboard');
    }

    let currentCulinaryModifier = {
      factorCals: 1.0,
      factorCarbs: 1.0,
      factorFats: 1.0,
      factorProt: 1.0,
      lossMicros: null,
      note: ''
    };

    function getAllFoods() {
      return [...EXTENDED_FOOD_DATABASE, ...appState.customFoods];
    }

    function handleFoodSearch(val) {
      const resultsDiv = document.getElementById('foodSearchResults');
      const query = (val || '').toLowerCase().trim();

      if (!query) {
        resultsDiv.classList.add('hidden');
        return;
      }

      const all = getAllFoods();
      const filtered = all.filter(f => f.name.toLowerCase().includes(query) || (f.category && f.category.toLowerCase().includes(query)));

      if (filtered.length === 0) {
        resultsDiv.innerHTML = `
          <div class="p-2.5 text-xs text-slate-400 text-center">
            Aucun aliment trouvé. 
            <button onclick="openCustomFoodModal('${val.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')" class="text-brand-400 underline font-semibold ml-1">Créer "${val}" ?</button>
          </div>
        `;
        resultsDiv.classList.remove('hidden');
        return;
      }

      resultsDiv.innerHTML = filtered.map(item => `
        <div onclick="selectFoodItem('${item.id}')" class="p-2 hover:bg-slate-800 rounded-lg cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-between text-xs transition">
          <div>
            <span class="font-bold text-slate-200 block">${item.name}</span>
            <span class="text-[10px] text-slate-400">${item.category || 'Aliment'} • ${item.per100g.cals} kcal/100g (P: ${item.per100g.p}g • G: ${item.per100g.c}g • L: ${item.per100g.f}g)</span>
          </div>
          <i class="fa-solid fa-angle-right text-slate-500 text-xs"></i>
        </div>
      `).join('');
      resultsDiv.classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function selectFoodItem(id) {
      const all = getAllFoods();
      const found = all.find(f => f.id === id);
      if (found) {
        selectedFoodObj = found;
        document.getElementById('activeFoodName').textContent = found.name;
        document.getElementById('activeFoodPer100').textContent = `${found.per100g.cals} kcal / 100g (P:${found.per100g.p}g)`;
        document.getElementById('foodSearchInput').value = '';
        document.getElementById('foodSearchResults').classList.add('hidden');
        updateFoodPreview();
      }
    }

    // Presets culinaires dédiés
    function setCookingPreset(preset) {
      // Nettoyer tous les boutons actifs
      document.querySelectorAll('.cooking-btn').forEach(b => {
        b.classList.remove('border-brand-500', 'bg-brand-500/20', 'text-brand-300');
        b.classList.add('border-slate-700/60', 'bg-slate-800', 'text-slate-300');
      });

      const customInput = document.getElementById('customCookingInput');
      const btn = document.getElementById(`btnPreset-${preset}`);

      // Si action de réinitialisation ou preset inconnu
      if (!btn || preset === 'reset_all') {
        currentCulinaryModifier = { factorCals: 1.0, factorCarbs: 1.0, factorFats: 1.0, factorProt: 1.0, lossMicros: null, note: '' };
        document.getElementById('culinaryStatusBadge').textContent = 'Aucune correction';
        document.getElementById('culinaryStatusBadge').className = 'text-[10px] bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-slate-700 font-semibold';
        document.getElementById('culinaryExplanation').textContent = 'Valeurs nutritionnelles brutes retenues à 100%.';
        if (customInput) customInput.value = '';
        updateFoodPreview();
        return;
      }

      const isAlreadyActive = btn.dataset.active === 'true';

      // Réinitialiser les états data-active sur tous les boutons
      document.querySelectorAll('.cooking-btn').forEach(b => {
        b.dataset.active = 'false';
      });

      if (isAlreadyActive) {
        currentCulinaryModifier = { factorCals: 1.0, factorCarbs: 1.0, factorFats: 1.0, factorProt: 1.0, lossMicros: null, note: '' };
        document.getElementById('culinaryStatusBadge').textContent = 'Aucune correction';
        document.getElementById('culinaryStatusBadge').className = 'text-[10px] bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-slate-700 font-semibold';
        document.getElementById('culinaryExplanation').textContent = 'Valeurs nutritionnelles brutes retenues à 100%.';
        if (customInput) customInput.value = '';
        updateFoodPreview();
        return;
      }

      btn.dataset.active = 'true';
      btn.classList.add('border-brand-500', 'bg-brand-500/20', 'text-brand-300');
      btn.classList.remove('border-slate-700/60', 'bg-slate-800', 'text-slate-300');

      if (preset === 'singeage') {
        currentCulinaryModifier = { factorCals: 0.25, factorCarbs: 0.25, factorFats: 0.25, factorProt: 0.25, lossMicros: null, note: '🌾 Farine excédentaire évier (-75%)' };
        document.getElementById('culinaryStatusBadge').textContent = 'Glucides & Cals -75%';
        document.getElementById('culinaryStatusBadge').className = 'text-[10px] bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/40 px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold';
        document.getElementById('culinaryExplanation').textContent = 'Singeage : seule une fine pellicule reste sur la viande, 75% est jeté dans l\'évier.';
        customInput.value = 'Farine résiduelle égouttée';
      } else if (preset === 'deglacage') {
        currentCulinaryModifier = { factorCals: 0.35, factorCarbs: 1.0, factorFats: 1.0, factorProt: 1.0, lossMicros: null, note: '🍷 Déglaçage vin (évap. alcool)' };
        document.getElementById('culinaryStatusBadge').textContent = 'Alcool évaporé (-65% kcal)';
        document.getElementById('culinaryStatusBadge').className = 'text-[10px] bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-300 border border-cyan-500/40 px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold';
        document.getElementById('culinaryExplanation').textContent = 'L\'éthanol bout à 78°C et s\'évapore à la réduction. Seuls les sucres et arômes sont comptés.';
        customInput.value = 'Réduction vin blanc';
      } else if (preset === 'degraissage') {
        currentCulinaryModifier = { factorCals: 0.75, factorCarbs: 1.0, factorFats: 0.60, factorProt: 1.0, lossMicros: null, note: '🍳 Dégraissage poêle (-40% L)' };
        document.getElementById('culinaryStatusBadge').textContent = 'Lipides -40%';
        document.getElementById('culinaryStatusBadge').className = 'text-[10px] bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/40 px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold';
        document.getElementById('culinaryExplanation').textContent = 'Poêle égouttée ou papier absorbant : 40% des graisses de cuisson fondues ne sont pas ingérées.';
        customInput.value = 'Graisses fondues égouttées';
      } else if (preset === 'ebullition') {
        currentCulinaryModifier = { factorCals: 1.0, factorCarbs: 1.0, factorFats: 1.0, factorProt: 1.0, lossMicros: ['vitC', 'vitB1', 'vitB9'], note: '💧 Eau jetée (lessivage vit hydrosolubles)' };
        document.getElementById('culinaryStatusBadge').textContent = 'Vitamines lessivées';
        document.getElementById('culinaryStatusBadge').className = 'text-[10px] bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-300 border border-purple-500/40 px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold';
        document.getElementById('culinaryExplanation').textContent = 'Cuisson à gros bouillons sans boire le bouillon : fuite de 40% des vitamines C, B1 et B9.';
        customInput.value = 'Eau de cuisson évacuée';
      }
      updateFoodPreview();
    }

    function analyzeCustomCooking(text) {
      const q = (text || '').toLowerCase().trim();
      const badge = document.getElementById('culinaryStatusBadge');
      const expl = document.getElementById('culinaryExplanation');

      if (!q) {
        currentCulinaryModifier = { factorCals: 1.0, factorCarbs: 1.0, factorFats: 1.0, factorProt: 1.0, lossMicros: null, note: '' };
        badge.textContent = 'Aucune correction';
        badge.className = 'text-[10px] bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-slate-700 font-semibold';
        expl.textContent = 'Valeurs nutritionnelles brutes retenues à 100%.';
        updateFoodPreview();
        return;
      }

      if (q.includes('sing') || q.includes('farine') || q.includes('évier') || q.includes('3/4')) {
        currentCulinaryModifier = { factorCals: 0.25, factorCarbs: 0.25, factorFats: 0.25, factorProt: 0.25, lossMicros: null, note: text };
        badge.textContent = 'Glucides & Cals -75%';
        badge.className = 'text-[10px] bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/40 px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold';
        expl.textContent = 'Déduction automatique de 75% des calories de panure ou farine.';
      } else if (q.includes('vin') || q.includes('déglac') || q.includes('alcool')) {
        currentCulinaryModifier = { factorCals: 0.35, factorCarbs: 1.0, factorFats: 1.0, factorProt: 1.0, lossMicros: null, note: text };
        badge.textContent = 'Alcool évaporé (-65% kcal)';
        badge.className = 'text-[10px] bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-300 border border-cyan-500/40 px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold';
        expl.textContent = 'Évaporation de l\'alcool calculée avec précision.';
      } else if (q.includes('égoutt') || q.includes('gras') || q.includes('dégraiss')) {
        currentCulinaryModifier = { factorCals: 0.75, factorCarbs: 1.0, factorFats: 0.60, factorProt: 1.0, lossMicros: null, note: text };
        badge.textContent = 'Lipides -40%';
        badge.className = 'text-[10px] bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/40 px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold';
        expl.textContent = 'Déduction de 40% des matières grasses fondues.';
      } else {
        currentCulinaryModifier = { factorCals: 1.0, factorCarbs: 1.0, factorFats: 1.0, factorProt: 1.0, lossMicros: null, note: text };
        badge.textContent = 'Note enregistrée';
        badge.className = 'text-[10px] bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-slate-700 font-medium';
        expl.textContent = 'Action conservée avec le plat.';
      }
      updateFoodPreview();
    }

    function updateFoodPreview() {
      const grams = Number(document.getElementById('foodGramsInput').value) || 100;
      const factor = (grams / 100);

      const cals = Math.round(selectedFoodObj.per100g.cals * factor * currentCulinaryModifier.factorCals);
      const p = (selectedFoodObj.per100g.p * factor * currentCulinaryModifier.factorProt).toFixed(1);
      const c = (selectedFoodObj.per100g.c * factor * currentCulinaryModifier.factorCarbs).toFixed(1);
      const f = (selectedFoodObj.per100g.f * factor * currentCulinaryModifier.factorFats).toFixed(1);

      document.getElementById('prevCals').textContent = cals;
      document.getElementById('prevP').textContent = `${p}g`;
      document.getElementById('prevC').textContent = `${c}g`;
      document.getElementById('prevF').textContent = `${f}g`;
    }

    function addSelectedFoodToJournal() {
      const grams = Number(document.getElementById('foodGramsInput').value) || 100;
      const meal = document.getElementById('mealTypeSelect').value;
      const factor = (grams / 100);

      const scaledMicros = {};
      if (selectedFoodObj.micros) {
        Object.keys(selectedFoodObj.micros).forEach(k => {
          let microFactor = factor;
          if (currentCulinaryModifier.lossMicros && currentCulinaryModifier.lossMicros.includes(k)) {
            microFactor *= 0.6; // -40% perte eau
          }
          scaledMicros[k] = selectedFoodObj.micros[k] * microFactor;
        });
      }

      appState.dailyLogs.push({
        id: 'food_' + Date.now(),
        name: selectedFoodObj.name,
        meal: meal,
        amount: `${grams}g`,
        cookingNote: currentCulinaryModifier.note || null,
        cals: Math.round(selectedFoodObj.per100g.cals * factor * currentCulinaryModifier.factorCals),
        p: Number((selectedFoodObj.per100g.p * factor * currentCulinaryModifier.factorProt).toFixed(1)),
        c: Number((selectedFoodObj.per100g.c * factor * currentCulinaryModifier.factorCarbs).toFixed(1)),
        f: Number((selectedFoodObj.per100g.f * factor * currentCulinaryModifier.factorFats).toFixed(1)),
        micros: scaledMicros
      });

      // Synchroniser avec la surveillance médicale si un nutriment correspond
      syncMedicalWatchlist(scaledMicros);

      // Reset preset
      setCookingPreset('reset_all');

      // Mettre à jour le marqueur de dernier repas pour le jeûne
      if (appState.fasting) {
        appState.fasting.lastMealTimestamp = Date.now();
      }

      saveState();
      renderAll();
    }

    /* Synchronisation avec les marqueurs de prise de sang */
    function syncMedicalWatchlist(foodMicros) {
      if (!foodMicros) return;
      appState.medicalWatchlist.forEach(mw => {
        if (mw.id === 'mw_fer' && foodMicros.fer) {
          mw.current = Number((mw.current + foodMicros.fer).toFixed(1));
        } else if (mw.id === 'mw_vitd' && foodMicros.vitD) {
          // 1 µg de Vit D = 40 UI
          mw.current = Math.round(mw.current + (foodMicros.vitD * 40));
        }
      });
    }

    /* =====================================================================
       6. CALCULS ÉNERGÉTIQUES & DÉPENSE MULTI-SPORTS
       ===================================================================== */
    function calculateBMR() {
      const p = appState.profile;
      if (p.bodyFat && p.bodyFat > 5) {
        const leanMass = p.weight * (1 - (p.bodyFat / 100));
        return Math.round(370 + (21.6 * leanMass));
      }
      let bmr = (10 * p.weight) + (6.25 * p.height) - (5 * p.age);
      bmr += (p.gender === 'male' ? 5 : -161);
      return Math.round(bmr);
    }

    function calculateTotalWorkoutCalories() {
      const weight = appState.profile.weight;
      if (appState.profile.isInjured) return 0; // Si blessé, zéro sport compté

      let total = 0;
      appState.todayActivities.forEach(act => {
        const burned = Math.round((act.met * 3.5 * weight / 200) * act.duration);
        act.burned = burned;
        total += burned;
      });
      return total;
    }

    function calculateTargets() {
      const bmr = calculateBMR();
      const sedentaryBase = bmr * (appState.profile.isInjured ? 1.15 : 1.25);
      const workoutBurn = calculateTotalWorkoutCalories();
      let tdee = sedentaryBase + workoutBurn;

      let targetCalories = tdee;
      if (appState.profile.isInjured) {
        // En convalescence, léger maintien strict
        targetCalories = tdee;
      } else if (appState.profile.goal === 'cut') {
        targetCalories = tdee * 0.83; // -17% pour définition des abdos
      } else if (appState.profile.goal === 'bulk') {
        targetCalories = tdee * 1.12;
      }
      targetCalories = Math.round(targetCalories);

      // Macros : 2.0 g/kg en protéines, 0.9 g/kg lipides
      const targetProt = Math.round(appState.profile.weight * (appState.profile.isInjured ? 2.2 : 2.0));
      const targetFats = Math.round(appState.profile.weight * 0.9);
      const calsFromPF = (targetProt * 4) + (targetFats * 9);
      const targetCarbs = Math.max(50, Math.round((targetCalories - calsFromPF) / 4));

      return { bmr, tdee: Math.round(tdee), targetCalories, targetProt, targetCarbs, targetFats, workoutBurn };
    }

    function toggleInjuryMode(checked) {
      appState.profile.isInjured = checked;
      saveState();
      renderAll();
    }

    function addStandardSportActivity() {
      const select = document.getElementById('sportSelectStandard');
      const [sportKey, metStr] = select.value.split(':');
      const sportName = select.options[select.selectedIndex].text.split(' (')[0];
      const duration = Number(document.getElementById('sportDurationStandard').value) || 60;

      appState.todayActivities.push({
        name: sportName,
        duration: duration,
        met: parseFloat(metStr),
        burned: 0
      });
      saveState();
      renderAll();
    }

    function addCustomSportActivity() {
      const name = document.getElementById('customSportName').value.trim();
      const met = parseFloat(document.getElementById('customSportIntensity').value) || 7.5;
      const duration = Number(document.getElementById('customSportDuration').value) || 45;

      if (!name) return;

      appState.todayActivities.push({
        name: name,
        duration: duration,
        met: met,
        burned: 0
      });

      document.getElementById('customSportName').value = '';
      saveState();
      renderAll();
    }

    function removeSportActivity(idx) {
      appState.todayActivities.splice(idx, 1);
      saveState();
      renderAll();
    }

    /* =====================================================================
       7. BANDEAU ADAPTATIF INTELLIGENT (ANALYSE DE LA VEILLE)
       ===================================================================== */
    function updateYesterdayAdvice(targets) {
      const yesterday = appState.historyDays[appState.historyDays.length - 1];
      const diff = yesterday.consumed - yesterday.target;
      const banner = document.getElementById('yesterdayAdviceBanner');
      const tag = document.getElementById('yesterdayTag');
      const text = document.getElementById('yesterdayAdviceText');

      tag.textContent = `Hier : ${yesterday.consumed.toLocaleString()} kcal (Cible : ${yesterday.target.toLocaleString()})`;

      if (diff > 250) {
        banner.className = "bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-900 via-amber-950/40 to-slate-900 border border-amber-500/40 rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-lg flex items-start gap-3.5";
        text.innerHTML = `
          Hier, vous avez consommé un excédent de <strong class="text-amber-300">+${diff} kcal</strong> (repas riche ou écart). 
          <strong>Stratégie d'équilibrage :</strong> Pas de jeûne drastique ! Pour préserver votre masse musculaire et votre objectif <em>Définition Abdos</em>, concentrez vos protéines ce midi (poulet, dinde, cabillaud) avec légumes en volume, et réduisez de 50g votre portion de féculents ce soir.
        `;
      } else if (diff < -300) {
        banner.className = "bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-900 via-cyan-950/40 to-slate-900 border border-cyan-500/40 rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-lg flex items-start gap-3.5";
        text.innerHTML = `
          Hier, vous étiez en sous-consommation de <strong class="text-cyan-300">${Math.abs(diff)} kcal</strong> par rapport à votre dépense sportive. 
          <strong>Risque :</strong> Fonte musculaire et fatigue nerveuse au rugby/course. Aujourd'hui, ajoutez une collation (flocons d'avoine + whey ou fromage blanc) pour recharger pleinement vos stocks de glycogène.
        `;
      } else {
        banner.className = "bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-900 via-emerald-950/40 to-slate-900 border border-brand-500/30 rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-lg flex items-start gap-3.5";
        text.innerHTML = `
          Excellente régularité ! Hier votre apport était parfaitement aligné sur votre cible (<strong class="text-emerald-400">écart de seulement ${Math.abs(diff)} kcal</strong>). 
          Poursuivez sur cette trajectoire : visez votre quota de <strong>${targets.targetProt}g de protéines</strong> pour stimuler la synthèse musculaire.
        `;
      }
    }

    /* =====================================================================
       8. RENDU GLOBAL & GRAPHIQUES
       ===================================================================== */
    let macrosChartInstance = null;
    let caloriesChartInstance = null;
    let databasePieChartInstance = null;

    /* Rendu du Camembert de répartition des 505 aliments */
    function renderDatabasePieChart() {
      const allFoods = getAllFoods();
      const categoryCounts = {};

      allFoods.forEach(food => {
        const cat = food.category || 'Autres';
        categoryCounts[cat] = (categoryCounts[cat] || 0) + 1;
      });

      const labels = Object.keys(categoryCounts);
      const dataValues = Object.values(categoryCounts);

      // Palette de couleurs raffinée
      const colorPalette = [
        '#10b981', '#06b6d4', '#f59e0b', '#f43f5e', '#8b5cf6', 
        '#3b82f6', '#ec4899', '#14b8a6', '#84cc16', '#eab308', 
        '#6366f1', '#a855f7', '#d946ef', '#64748b', '#0ea5e9'
      ];

      // Mise à jour de la légende détaillée
      const legendContainer = document.getElementById('databaseCategoryLegend');
      if (legendContainer) {
        legendContainer.innerHTML = labels.map((cat, idx) => {
          const count = categoryCounts[cat];
          const pct = ((count / allFoods.length) * 100).toFixed(1);
          const color = colorPalette[idx % colorPalette.length];
          return `
            <div class="p-2.5 bg-slate-900/70 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span class="w-3 h-3 rounded-full shrink-0" style="background-color: ${color}"></span>
                <span class="text-slate-200 font-semibold truncate max-w-[150px]">${cat}</span>
              </div>
              <div class="text-right">
                <span class="font-bold text-white">${count}</span>
                <span class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 ml-1">(${pct}%)</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          `;
        }).join('');
      }

      // Rendu Chart.js Camembert (Pie)
      const canvas = document.getElementById('databasePieChart');
      if (!canvas) return;

      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
      if (databasePieChartInstance) databasePieChartInstance.destroy();

      databasePieChartInstance = new Chart(ctx, {
        type: 'pie',
        data: {
          labels: labels,
          datasets: [{
            data: dataValues,
            backgroundColor: labels.map((_, i) => colorPalette[i % colorPalette.length]),
            borderColor: '#090d16',
            borderWidth: 2
          }]
        },
        options: {
          responsive: true,
          maintainAspectRatio: false,
          plugins: {
            legend: {
              display: false
            },
            tooltip: {
              callbacks: {
                label: function(context) {
                  const label = context.label || '';
                  const value = context.parsed || 0;
                  const pct = ((value / allFoods.length) * 100).toFixed(1);
                  return ` ${label} : ${value} aliments (${pct}%)`;
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }
      });
    }

    function renderAll() {
      const targets = calculateTargets();
      
      // Calcul totaux
      let totals = { cals: 0, p: 0, c: 0, f: 0, micros: {} };
      [...ESSENTIAL_MICRONUTRIENTS.vitamins, ...ESSENTIAL_MICRONUTRIENTS.minerals].forEach(m => totals.micros[m.id] = 0);

      appState.dailyLogs.forEach(log => {
        totals.cals += log.cals;
        totals.p += log.p;
        totals.c += log.c;
        totals.f += log.f;
        if (log.micros) {
          Object.keys(log.micros).forEach(k => {
            if (totals.micros[k] !== undefined) totals.micros[k] += log.micros[k];
          });
        }
      });

      totals.cals = Math.round(totals.cals);
      totals.p = Math.round(totals.p);
      totals.c = Math.round(totals.c);
      totals.f = Math.round(totals.f);

      // Header
      document.getElementById('navTargetCalories').textContent = `${targets.targetCalories.toLocaleString()} kcal`;
      document.getElementById('navConsumedCalories').textContent = `${totals.cals.toLocaleString()} kcal`;

      // Bandeau Veille
      updateYesterdayAdvice(targets);

      // Dashboard
      document.getElementById('statTargetCals').textContent = targets.targetCalories.toLocaleString();
      document.getElementById('statConsumedCals').textContent = totals.cals.toLocaleString();
      const rem = targets.targetCalories - totals.cals;
      document.getElementById('statRemainingCals').textContent = rem >= 0 ? `Reste : ${rem.toLocaleString()} kcal` : `Dépassement : ${Math.abs(rem).toLocaleString()} kcal`;
      document.getElementById('barCalories').style.width = `${Math.min(100, Math.round((totals.cals / targets.targetCalories) * 100))}%`;

      // Protéines
      document.getElementById('statTargetProt').textContent = targets.targetProt;
      document.getElementById('statConsumedProt').textContent = totals.p;
      const protPct = Math.min(100, Math.round((totals.p / targets.targetProt) * 100));
      document.getElementById('barProtein').style.width = `${protPct}%`;
      document.getElementById('statProtSub').textContent = `${protPct}% atteint (${(totals.p / appState.profile.weight).toFixed(1)} g/kg)`;

      // Glucides
      document.getElementById('statTargetCarbs').textContent = targets.targetCarbs;
      document.getElementById('statConsumedCarbs').textContent = totals.c;
      document.getElementById('barCarbs').style.width = `${Math.min(100, Math.round((totals.c / targets.targetCarbs) * 100))}%`;

      // Lipides
      document.getElementById('statTargetFats').textContent = targets.targetFats;
      document.getElementById('statConsumedFats').textContent = totals.f;
      document.getElementById('barFats').style.width = `${Math.min(100, Math.round((totals.f / targets.targetFats) * 100))}%`;

      // Ratios
      const macroSumCals = (totals.p * 4) + (totals.c * 4) + (totals.f * 9);
      if (macroSumCals > 0) {
        document.getElementById('ratioProt').textContent = `${Math.round(((totals.p * 4) / macroSumCals) * 100)}%`;
        document.getElementById('ratioCarbs').textContent = `${Math.round(((totals.c * 4) / macroSumCals) * 100)}%`;
        document.getElementById('ratioFats').textContent = `${Math.round(((totals.f * 9) / macroSumCals) * 100)}%`;
      } else {
        document.getElementById('ratioProt').textContent = '0%';
        document.getElementById('ratioCarbs').textContent = '0%';
        document.getElementById('ratioFats').textContent = '0%';
      }

      // Journal items
      renderJournalList();

      // 27 Micronutriments
      renderMicronutrients(totals.micros);

      // Marqueurs de santé
      renderMedicalWatchlist();

      // Profil
      document.getElementById('valBMR').textContent = targets.bmr.toLocaleString();
      document.getElementById('valTDEE').textContent = targets.tdee.toLocaleString();
      document.getElementById('badgeTotalWorkoutBurned').textContent = `+${targets.workoutBurn} kcal`;
      renderActivitiesList();

      // Compléments
      renderSupplementsList();

      // Charts
      renderCharts(totals, targets);
      renderDatabasePieChart();

      // Hydratation & Jeûne
      renderWaterUI();
      updateFastingTimerUI();

      // Métriques corporelles
      document.getElementById('statBodyWeight').textContent = (appState.profile.weight || 78).toFixed(1);
      document.getElementById('statWaistCircumference').textContent = (appState.waistCircumference || 81.5).toFixed(1);
      document.getElementById('statBodyFatDisplay').textContent = (appState.profile.bodyFat || 13.5).toFixed(1);
    }

    function renderJournalList() {
      const list = document.getElementById('journalItemsList');
      if (appState.dailyLogs.length === 0) {
        list.innerHTML = `<p class="text-xs text-slate-500 italic py-8 text-center">Aucun aliment consigné aujourd'hui. Tapez un aliment dans la recherche ci-contre pour commencer.</p>`;
        return;
      }

      list.innerHTML = appState.dailyLogs.map((item, idx) => `
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between p-2.5 bg-slate-900/60 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 text-xs">
          <div>
            <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-wrap">
              <span class="font-bold text-slate-200">${item.name}</span>
              <span class="text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 border border-slate-700">${item.meal}</span>
              ${item.brand ? `<span class="text-[10px] text-brand-400 font-semibold">(${item.brand})</span>` : ''}
              ${item.cookingNote ? `<span class="text-[10px] text-amber-300 bg-amber-950/40 border border-amber-600/30 px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded" title="Action culinaire : ${item.cookingNote}"><i class="fa-solid fa-wand-magic-sparkles mr-1"></i>${item.cookingNote}</span>` : ''}
            </div>
            <p class="text-slate-400 text-[11px] mt-0.5">${item.amount} • <strong class="text-white">${item.cals} kcal</strong> | P: ${item.p}g • G: ${item.c}g • L: ${item.f}g</p>
          </div>
          <button onclick="removeJournalItem(${idx})" class="text-slate-500 hover:text-red-400 p-1.5 transition">
            <i class="fa-solid fa-xmark"></i>
          </button>
        </div>
      `).join('');
    }

    function removeJournalItem(idx) {
      appState.dailyLogs.splice(idx, 1);
      saveState();
      renderAll();
    }

    function clearJournalToday() {
      if (confirm('Effacer tous les repas consommés aujourd\'hui ?')) {
        appState.dailyLogs = [];
        saveState();
        renderAll();
      }
    }

    function renderMicronutrients(micros) {
      const renderGroup = (list) => list.map(m => {
        const val = micros[m.id] ? Number(micros[m.id].toFixed(1)) : 0;
        const pct = Math.round((val / m.rda) * 100);

        let color = 'bg-rose-500';
        let badge = 'text-rose-400 bg-rose-950/40 border-rose-800/40';
        if (pct >= 100) {
          color = 'bg-brand-400';
          badge = 'text-brand-400 bg-emerald-950/40 border-emerald-800/40';
        } else if (pct >= 50) {
          color = 'bg-amber-400';
          badge = 'text-amber-400 bg-amber-950/40 border-amber-800/40';
        }

        return `
          <div class="p-3 bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl space-y-1.5">
            <div class="flex items-start justify-between">
              <div>
                <span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-200 block">${m.name}</span>
                <span class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 line-clamp-1" title="${m.desc}">${m.desc}</span>
              </div>
              <span class="text-[10px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded border ${badge}">${pct}%</span>
            </div>
            <div class="w-full bg-slate-800 rounded-full h-1.5 overflow-hidden">
              <div class="${color} h-1.5 rounded-full gauge-fill" style="width: ${Math.min(100, pct)}%"></div>
            </div>
            <div class="flex justify-between text-[10px] text-slate-400">
              <span>Apport : <strong class="text-slate-200">${val}</strong> ${m.unit}</span>
              <span>RNP : ${m.rda} ${m.unit}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }).join('');

      document.getElementById('vitaminsGrid').innerHTML = renderGroup(ESSENTIAL_MICRONUTRIENTS.vitamins);
      document.getElementById('mineralsGrid').innerHTML = renderGroup(ESSENTIAL_MICRONUTRIENTS.minerals);
    }

    function renderMedicalWatchlist() {
      const grid = document.getElementById('medicalWatchlistGrid');
      if (appState.medicalWatchlist.length === 0) {
        grid.innerHTML = `<p class="text-xs text-slate-500 italic py-2 col-span-3">Aucun marqueur médical spécifique configuré.</p>`;
        return;
      }

      grid.innerHTML = appState.medicalWatchlist.map((mw, idx) => {
        const pct = Math.min(100, Math.round((mw.current / mw.target) * 100));
        return `
          <div class="p-3 bg-slate-900/80 border border-purple-500/30 rounded-xl space-y-2">
            <div class="flex items-start justify-between">
              <div>
                <span class="text-xs font-bold text-purple-300 block">${mw.name}</span>
                <span class="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Objectif suivi : ${mw.target} ${mw.unit}</span>
              </div>
              <button onclick="removeMedicalMarker(${idx})" class="text-slate-500 hover:text-red-400 text-xs">
                <i class="fa-solid fa-xmark"></i>
              </button>
            </div>
            <div class="w-full bg-slate-800 rounded-full h-2 overflow-hidden">
              <div class="bg-gradient-to-r from-accentViolet to-brand-400 h-2 rounded-full gauge-fill" style="width: ${pct}%"></div>
            </div>
            <div class="flex justify-between text-[11px] items-center">
              <span class="text-slate-300">Reçu : <strong>${mw.current}</strong> ${mw.unit}</span>
              <button onclick="incrementMarker(${idx})" class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-purple-900/50 hover:bg-purple-800 text-purple-200 text-[10px] border border-purple-500/30">
                + Ajouter dose
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }).join('');
    }

    function incrementMarker(idx) {
      const mw = appState.medicalWatchlist[idx];
      const addVal = prompt(`Quantité de ${mw.name} à ajouter (${mw.unit}) :`, "1000");
      if (addVal && !isNaN(addVal)) {
        mw.current = Number((mw.current + parseFloat(addVal)).toFixed(1));
        saveState();
        renderAll();
      }
    }

    function removeMedicalMarker(idx) {
      appState.medicalWatchlist.splice(idx, 1);
      saveState();
      renderAll();
    }

    function renderActivitiesList() {
      const list = document.getElementById('todayActivitiesList');
      if (appState.todayActivities.length === 0) {
        list.innerHTML = `<p class="text-xs text-slate-500 italic py-2">Aucune activité sportive enregistrée aujourd'hui.</p>`;
        return;
      }

      list.innerHTML = appState.todayActivities.map((act, idx) => `
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between p-2 bg-slate-900/50 border border-slate-800 rounded-lg text-xs">
          <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
            <i class="fa-solid fa-dumbbell text-accentAmber text-xs"></i>
            <span class="text-slate-200 font-medium">${act.name}</span>
            <span class="text-slate-400">(${act.duration} min • MET ${act.met})</span>
          </div>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
            <span class="text-amber-400 font-bold">+${act.burned} kcal</span>
            <button onclick="removeSportActivity(${idx})" class="text-slate-500 hover:text-red-400">
              <i class="fa-solid fa-trash-can"></i>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      `).join('');
    }

    function renderSupplementsList() {
      const all = [...DEFAULT_SUPPLEMENTS, ...appState.customSupplements];
      const grid = document.getElementById('supplementsCatalogueGrid');

      grid.innerHTML = all.map(s => `
        <div class="p-3 bg-slate-900/60 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 flex flex-col justify-between space-y-2.5">
          <div>
            <div class="flex items-start justify-between">
              <div>
                <span class="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-brand-400">${s.brand}</span>
                <h4 class="text-xs font-bold text-white">${s.name}</h4>
              </div>
              <span class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 bg-slate-800 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">${s.doseUnit}</span>
            </div>
            <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-1">${s.cals} kcal | P: ${s.p}g • G: ${s.c}g • L: ${s.f}g</p>
          </div>
          <button onclick="addSupplementToLog('${s.id}')" class="w-full py-1.5 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-brand-500/20 hover:text-brand-400 hover:border-brand-500/40 border border-slate-700 transition rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
            <i class="fa-solid fa-plus text-[10px]"></i> Consommer
          </button>
        </div>
      `).join('');
    }

    function addSupplementToLog(id) {
      const all = [...DEFAULT_SUPPLEMENTS, ...appState.customSupplements];
      const supp = all.find(s => s.id === id);
      if (!supp) return;

      appState.dailyLogs.push({
        id: 'supp_' + Date.now(),
        name: supp.name,
        brand: supp.brand,
        meal: 'Complément',
        amount: supp.doseUnit,
        cookingNote: null,
        cals: supp.cals,
        p: supp.p,
        c: supp.c,
        f: supp.f,
        micros: supp.micros || {}
      });

      syncMedicalWatchlist(supp.micros);
      saveState();
      renderAll();
    }

    function handleSaveCustomSupplement(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const name = document.getElementById('custSuppName').value.trim();
      const brand = document.getElementById('custSuppBrand').value.trim();
      const dose = document.getElementById('custSuppDose').value.trim();
      const cals = Number(document.getElementById('custSuppCals').value) || 0;
      const p = Number(document.getElementById('custSuppP').value) || 0;
      const c = Number(document.getElementById('custSuppC').value) || 0;
      const f = Number(document.getElementById('custSuppF').value) || 0;

      const microKey = document.getElementById('custSuppMicroKey').value;
      const microVal = Number(document.getElementById('custSuppMicroVal').value) || 0;

      const micros = {};
      if (microKey && microVal > 0) micros[microKey] = microVal;

      appState.customSupplements.push({
        id: 'cust_supp_' + Date.now(),
        name, brand, doseUnit: dose, cals, p, c, f, micros
      });

      saveState();
      renderSupplementsList();
      e.target.reset();
    }

    function renderCharts(totals, targets) {
      // Donut Chart
      const donutCtx = document.getElementById('macrosDonutChart')?.getContext('2d');
      if (donutCtx) {
        if (macrosChartInstance) macrosChartInstance.destroy();
        const pCals = totals.p * 4;
        const cCals = totals.c * 4;
        const fCals = totals.f * 9;
        const hasData = (pCals + cCals + fCals) > 0;

        macrosChartInstance = new Chart(donutCtx, {
          type: 'doughnut',
          data: {
            labels: ['Protéines', 'Glucides', 'Lipides'],
            datasets: [{
              data: hasData ? [pCals, cCals, fCals] : [1, 1, 1],
              backgroundColor: hasData ? ['#34d399', '#06b6d4', '#f43f5e'] : ['#334155', '#334155', '#334155'],
              borderWidth: 2,
              borderColor: '#111827'
            }]
          },
          options: {
            responsive: true,
            maintainAspectRatio: false,
            plugins: {
              legend: { position: 'bottom', labels: { color: '#94a3b8', boxWidth: 12 } }
            },
            cutout: '70%'
          }
        });
      }

      // Bar Chart Calories 7 jours
      const barCtx = document.getElementById('caloriesHistoryChart')?.getContext('2d');
      if (barCtx) {
        if (caloriesChartInstance) caloriesChartInstance.destroy();

        const labels = [...appState.historyDays.map(d => d.date), 'Aujourd\'hui'];
        const dataConsumed = [...appState.historyDays.map(d => d.consumed), totals.cals];
        const dataTarget = [...appState.historyDays.map(d => d.target), targets.targetCalories];

        caloriesChartInstance = new Chart(barCtx, {
          type: 'bar',
          data: {
            labels: labels,
            datasets: [
              {
                label: 'Consommé (kcal)',
                data: dataConsumed,
                backgroundColor: dataConsumed.map((val, idx) => {
                  const tgt = dataTarget[idx];
                  return Math.abs(val - tgt) < 200 ? '#10b981' : (val > tgt ? '#f43f5e' : '#f59e0b');
                }),
                borderRadius: 6
              },
              {
                label: 'Cible Athlète',
                data: dataTarget,
                type: 'line',
                borderColor: '#38bdf8',
                borderDash: [5, 5],
                pointRadius: 0,
                fill: false
              }
            ]
          },
          options: {
            responsive: true,
            maintainAspectRatio: false,
            scales: {
              x: { grid: { display: false }, ticks: { color: '#94a3b8' } },
              y: { grid: { color: '#1e293b' }, ticks: { color: '#94a3b8' } }
            },
            plugins: {
              legend: { position: 'bottom', labels: { color: '#94a3b8', boxWidth: 12 } }
            }
          }
        });
      }
    }

    /* =====================================================================
       9. MODALS & ONGLETS
       ===================================================================== */
    function openCustomFoodModal(prefill = '') {
      document.getElementById('customFoodModal').classList.remove('hidden');
      if (prefill) document.getElementById('newFoodName').value = prefill;
    }
    function closeCustomFoodModal() {
      document.getElementById('customFoodModal').classList.add('hidden');
    }
    function handleCreateCustomFood(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const name = document.getElementById('newFoodName').value.trim();
      const cals = Number(document.getElementById('newFoodCals').value) || 0;
      const p = Number(document.getElementById('newFoodP').value) || 0;
      const c = Number(document.getElementById('newFoodC').value) || 0;
      const f = Number(document.getElementById('newFoodF').value) || 0;

      const newFood = {
        id: 'cust_food_' + Date.now(),
        name,
        category: 'Perso',
        per100g: { cals, p, c, f },
        micros: {}
      };

      appState.customFoods.push(newFood);
      saveState();
      closeCustomFoodModal();
      selectFoodItem(newFood.id);
      e.target.reset();
    }

    function openCustomMarkerModal() {
      document.getElementById('customMarkerModal').classList.remove('hidden');
    }
    function closeCustomMarkerModal() {
      document.getElementById('customMarkerModal').classList.add('hidden');
    }
    function handleCreateCustomMarker(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const name = document.getElementById('markerName').value.trim();
      const target = Number(document.getElementById('markerTarget').value) || 100;
      const unit = document.getElementById('markerUnit').value.trim();
      const current = Number(document.getElementById('markerCurrent').value) || 0;

      appState.medicalWatchlist.push({
        id: 'mw_' + Date.now(),
        name, target, unit, current
      });

      saveState();
      closeCustomMarkerModal();
      renderMedicalWatchlist();
      e.target.reset();
    }

    function saveUserProfile() {
      appState.profile.gender = document.getElementById('profGender').value;
      appState.profile.age = Number(document.getElementById('profAge').value) || 24;
      appState.profile.weight = Number(document.getElementById('profWeight').value) || 78;
      appState.profile.height = Number(document.getElementById('profHeight').value) || 180;
      appState.profile.bodyFat = Number(document.getElementById('profBodyFat').value) || null;
      appState.profile.goal = document.getElementById('profGoal').value;

      saveState();
      renderAll();
    }

    function switchTab(tabId) {
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(el => el.classList.add('hidden'));
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(btn => {
        btn.classList.remove('bg-brand-500/15', 'text-brand-400', 'border-brand-500/30');
        btn.classList.add('text-slate-400', 'border-transparent');
      });

      const activeContent = document.getElementById(`tab-${tabId}`);
      const activeBtn = document.getElementById(`tabBtn-${tabId}`);
      if (activeContent) activeContent.classList.remove('hidden');
      if (activeBtn) {
        activeBtn.classList.remove('text-slate-400', 'border-transparent');
        activeBtn.classList.add('bg-brand-500/15', 'text-brand-400', 'border-brand-500/30');
      }

      if (tabId === 'analytics') {
        renderAll();
      } else if (tabId === 'database-view') {
        renderDatabasePieChart();
      }
    }

    // Clic en dehors pour fermer les résultats de recherche
    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      const searchBox = document.getElementById('foodSearchInput');
      const resultsDiv = document.getElementById('foodSearchResults');
      if (searchBox && resultsDiv && !searchBox.contains(e.target) && !resultsDiv.contains(e.target)) {
        resultsDiv.classList.add('hidden');
      }
    });

    window.onload = function() {
      loadState();

      // Synchronisation formulaire profil
      document.getElementById('profGender').value = appState.profile.gender;
      document.getElementById('profAge').value = appState.profile.age;
      document.getElementById('profWeight').value = appState.profile.weight;
      document.getElementById('profHeight').value = appState.profile.height;
      if (appState.profile.bodyFat) document.getElementById('profBodyFat').value = appState.profile.bodyFat;
      document.getElementById('profGoal').value = appState.profile.goal;
      document.getElementById('profInjuryToggle').checked = !!appState.profile.isInjured;

      selectFoodItem('poulet');
      renderAll();

      // Intervalle régulier pour actualiser le compteur du jeûne
      setInterval(updateFastingTimerUI, 60000);
    };
  </script>
</body>
</html>
